# RFC Requirement Ledger

GENERATED by `make ze-rfc-index` -- do not edit. Requirement text is authored in `rfc/short/*.md`; the test links are derived from `RFC requirement:` tags in the tests themselves (`ai/rules/derive-not-hardcode.md`).

4232 requirements across 172 summaries. 2711 are MUST-level; 92 of those are enrolled and gated by `make ze-rfc-check`.

An RFC is **enrolled** (`rfc/enrolled.txt`) when every MUST-level requirement it declares is covered by a positive AND a negative test, or annotated. Un-enrolled RFCs are listed here but not gated: that remainder is tracked, not hidden (`ai/rules/testing.md`, Back-Fill New Test Types).

## RFC1997 -- enrolled (gated)

| Requirement | Level | § | Positive test | Negative test | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RFC1997-Aggregation-1` | SHOULD | Aggregation | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC1997-Encoding-1` | MUST | Encoding | `internal/core/bgp/attribute/community_test.go:44` | `internal/core/bgp/attribute/community_test.go:54` |  |
| `RFC1997-Operation-1` | MAY | Operation | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC1997-Operation-2` | MAY | Operation | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC1997-Operation-3` | MAY | Operation | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC1997-Well-1` | MUST NOT | Well | -- | -- | {gap} not auto-enforced -- the egress gate writeUpdateGated (internal/component/bgp/reactor/session_write.go:263) consults only the peer's operator-configured export filter chain and never inspects community values, so a received route carrying NO_EXPORT (0xFFFFFF01) is re-advertised unless an operator adds a community-match reject entry (internal/component/bgp/plugins/filter_community_match); the RS control-community parser parseCommunityAttr (internal/component/bgp/wireu/community.go:113) matches only 0:X, rs-asn:X and 65535:666, never the well-known values. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 1997 row |
| `RFC1997-Well-2` | MUST NOT | Well | -- | -- | {gap} not auto-enforced -- same root cause as Well-1: writeUpdateGated (internal/component/bgp/reactor/session_write.go:263) never inspects communities, so a route carrying NO_ADVERTISE (0xFFFFFF02) is re-advertised to peers absent an operator community-match reject entry. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 1997 row |
| `RFC1997-Well-3` | MUST NOT | Well | -- | -- | {gap} not auto-enforced -- same root cause as Well-1: writeUpdateGated (internal/component/bgp/reactor/session_write.go:263) never inspects communities, so a route carrying NO_EXPORT_SUBCONFED (0xFFFFFF03) is re-advertised to external peers absent an operator community-match reject entry. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 1997 row |
| `RFC1997-Well-4` | SHALL | Well | -- | -- | {gap} the well-known egress operations are not auto-applied -- ze is community-attribute-aware for parsing, formatting and policy matching (ParseCommunities internal/core/bgp/attribute/community.go:201; filter_community_match) but honors NO_EXPORT/NO_ADVERTISE/NO_EXPORT_SUBCONFED only through operator-configured policy, never automatically; same root cause as Well-1..3. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 1997 row |

## RFC4360 -- enrolled (gated)

| Requirement | Level | § | Positive test | Negative test | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RFC4360-2-1` | MUST | 2 | `internal/core/bgp/attribute/community_test.go:297` | `internal/core/bgp/attribute/community_test.go:299` |  |
| `RFC4360-6-1` | MUST NOT | 6 | -- | -- | {not-applicable} ze's best-path decision (internal/component/bgp/plugins/rib/bestpath.go) selects on the RFC 4271 tie-breakers only -- LOCAL_PREF, AS_PATH length, ORIGIN, MED, ..., Router ID -- and never reads the Extended Communities attribute, so there is no ext-comm-driven path selection that could create a forwarding loop |
| `RFC4360-6-2` | SHOULD | 6 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC4360-6-3` | SHOULD NOT | 6 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC4360-6-4` | MAY | 6 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC4360-6-5` | MAY | 6 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC4360-7-1` | MUST NOT | 7 | -- | -- | {not-applicable} this constrains the IANA Extended Community type-code registry allocation, not a BGP implementation; ze consumes registry type codes and does not allocate them |
| `RFC4360-7-2` | MUST NOT | 7 | -- | -- | {not-applicable} same as 7-1 -- an IANA type-allocation rule on the registry, not implementation behavior; ze does not allocate Extended Community type codes |
| `RFC4360-x-1` | MUST | x | `internal/core/bgp/attribute/community_test.go:277` | `internal/core/bgp/attribute/community_test.go:287` |  |
| `RFC4360-x-2` | MUST | x | -- | -- | {not-applicable} this governs a speaker that does NOT support Extended Communities passing the optional-transitive attribute through unchanged (RFC 4271); ze supports the attribute -- it parses and re-encodes it, ExtendedCommunities.Flags is optional-transitive (internal/core/bgp/attribute/community.go:244) -- so ze is a supporting speaker, and the general RFC 4271 optional-transitive pass-through is tracked under RFC 4271/7606 |

## RFC6811 -- enrolled (gated)

| Requirement | Level | § | Positive test | Negative test | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RFC6811-2-1` | MUST | 2 | `internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/validate_test.go:16` | `internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/validate_test.go:31` |  |
| `RFC6811-2-2` | MUST NOT | 2 | -- | -- | {gap} when RPKI validation is active ze auto-rejects Invalid routes unconditionally -- buildDecisions computes `reject := req.state == ValidationInvalid` (internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/rpki.go:643) and dispatches a reject to adj-rib-in, with no configuration to retain Invalid routes for policy-based handling (unlike ASPA, which offers reject/log-only/accept, rpki_config.go:24-32). RFC 6811 requires exclusion to be explicitly configured via policy rather than an automatic side effect of the Invalid state. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 6811 row |
| `RFC6811-2-3` | SHOULD | 2 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC6811-2-4` | SHOULD | 2 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC6811-2-5` | SHOULD | 2 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC6811-2-6` | MAY | 2 | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC6811-3-1` | MUST | 3 | -- | -- | {gap} the validation state (Valid/Invalid/NotFound) is internal Adj-RIB-In metadata (internal/component/bgp/plugins/adj_rib_in/rib_validation.go:15-21) and is not exposed to the route-policy language -- the filter-chain attribute vocabulary (internal/component/bgp/reactor/filter_chain.go:30-68) has no validation-state key to match on, and no filter/modify setter assigns it, so an operator cannot match or set the validation state in policy. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 6811 row |
| `RFC6811-3-2` | MUST | 3 | `internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/validate_test.go:92` | `internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/validate_test.go:94` |  |
| `RFC6811-4-1` | MUST | 4 | -- | -- | {gap} when the RTR cache delivers a VRP mapping change, ROACache.ApplyDelta (internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/roa_cache.go:172) mutates silently with no change-set and no callback; the End-of-Data handler re-validates only ASPA-affected routes (onASPAChange, internal/component/bgp/plugins/rpki/rtr_session.go:322) and never re-runs origin Validate() over installed routes -- there is no origin route tracker or onROAChange trigger. Installed routes keep their pre-change validation state until an operator manually issues `request bgp adj-rib-in revalidate`. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 6811 row |

| `RFC7606-5.1-1` | MUST | 5.1 | -- | -- | {gap} Ze intentionally emits MP_UNREACH first and MP_REACH last, treating them as NLRI-carrying wire structures rather than descriptive attributes; deliberate design decision recorded in docs/architecture/wire/mp-nlri-ordering.md and disclosed publicly in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 7606 row |
| `RFC7606-5.1-2` | MUST NOT | 5.1 | -- | -- | {gap} Ze emits UPDATEs carrying more than one NLRI-bearing field -- buildUpdatePayload is documented as building a body with "any combination of components" and its callers pass all four (wireu/split.go:440, :194, :281), with split_test.go:1014 exercising non-empty Withdrawn Routes together with non-empty NLRI. Same family as the 5.1-1 divergence: 5.1's encoding restrictions exist to help a RECEIVER locate the NLRI field, and Ze forwards a received UPDATE's shape rather than re-encoding it. Disclosed in the docs/features/rfc-status.md RFC 7606 row |
| `RFC7606-5.1-3` | MUST | 5.1 | `internal/component/bgp/message/rfc7606_test.go:662` | -- | {single-polarity} the obligation is to ACCEPT MP attributes in any position, so the only conforming assertion is acceptance -- a negative would assert a rejection the RFC forbids |

## RFC792 -- enrolled (gated)

| Requirement | Level | § | Positive test | Negative test | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RFC792-Echo-1` | MUST | Echo | `internal/core/probe/icmp_test.go:71` | -- | {single-polarity} ze emits only echo requests and never an ICMP message of another type, so there is no ze-produced echo of a different type to assert against |
| `RFC792-Echo-10` | MAY | Echo | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC792-Echo-2` | MUST | Echo | `internal/core/probe/icmp_test.go:78` | -- | {single-polarity} ze emits Code 0 and never varies it, so there is no non-zero-code echo it produces to assert against |
| `RFC792-Echo-3` | MUST | Echo | `internal/core/probe/icmp_test.go:85` | `internal/core/probe/icmp_test.go:94` |  |
| `RFC792-Echo-4` | MUST | Echo | `internal/core/probe/icmp_test.go:104` | -- | {single-polarity} the zero pad is an internal step of a correct computation and ze rejects nothing on this basis, so only the positive direction is assertable |
| `RFC792-Echo-5` | MUST | Echo | -- | -- | {not-applicable} ze issues echo requests and consumes replies; it is not an ICMP echo responder, so returning the request data unchanged is the remote host's obligation, which ze relies on but does not implement |
| `RFC792-Echo-6` | MUST | Echo | -- | -- | {not-applicable} reply formation is the responder's role; ze does not answer inbound echo requests |
| `RFC792-Echo-7` | SHOULD | Echo | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC792-Echo-8` | MAY | Echo | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC792-Echo-9` | MAY | Echo | -- | -- |  |

## RFC7947 -- enrolled (gated)

| Requirement | Level | § | Positive test | Negative test | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RFC7947-x-1` | MUST NOT | x | `internal/component/bgp/reactor/forward_rs_test.go:395` | `internal/component/bgp/reactor/forward_rs_test.go:287` |  |
| `RFC7947-x-2` | MUST NOT | x | `internal/component/bgp/reactor/forward_rs_test.go:397` | -- | {single-polarity} NEXT_HOP transparency is not RS-specific -- every forwarded route preserves it by default (nhModeNone, internal/component/bgp/reactor/peer_forward_facts.go:147), so unlike x-1's AS-path prepend there is no "confined" negative where a comparable non-RS peer rewrites NEXT_HOP; the only rewrite is an explicit per-peer next-hop-self/explicit override, which exercises the override feature rather than the RS-transparency MUST-NOT. The positive is proven byte-identical in TestReactorForwardRSTransparent |
| `RFC7947-x-3` | MUST | x | `internal/component/bgp/reactor/forward_rs_test.go:399` | -- | {single-polarity} MULTI_EXIT_DISC (attribute type 4) is never modified on any RS forwarding path -- no mods.Op(4, ...) exists in the reactor -- so MED rides through in the verbatim wire and there is no MED-rewrite behavior to assert as a negative. The positive is proven byte-identical in TestReactorForwardRSTransparent |
| `RFC7947-x-4` | MUST | x | `internal/component/bgp/reactor/forward_rs_test.go:215` | `internal/component/bgp/reactor/forward_rs_test.go:134` |  |
| `RFC7947-x-5` | SHOULD | x | -- | -- |  |
| `RFC7947-x-6` | MAY | x | -- | -- |  |

## Summaries declaring no MUST-level requirement

These summaries contribute nothing to the ledger. That is correct for a genuinely non-normative reference, and a capture failure for anything else. The source column is the count of MUST/MUST NOT/SHALL/SHALL NOT in the RFC's own text: a non-zero source count with no captured requirement means the summary needs re-authoring (`/ze-rfc <stem>`) before its RFC can ever be enrolled.

| Summary | MUST-level keywords in source | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| `rfc1035` | 0 | consistent: source declares none |
| `rfc6987` | 0 | consistent: source declares none |
